# Graph Report - shiny-soaring-nest  (2026-08-04)

## Corpus Check
- 464 files · ~376,657 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 12631 nodes · 12854 edges · 2124 communities (872 shown, 1252 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 153 edges (avg confidence: 0.71)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `9a8eb396`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
